\title{\textbf{The Integrated Cycle and Categorical Framework for Uniruled Fourfolds}}
\author{\textbf{Formalized Blueprint and Global Verification Structure}}
\date{October 2026}

\begin{document}

\maketitle

\begin{abstract}
We present a complete mathematical blueprint and formalization schema for the cycle and categorical structures of uniruled complex projective fourfolds. This framework links the Bloch--Srinivas decomposition of the diagonal to the semi-orthogonal decompositions of bounded derived categories, Bridgeland stability conditions, Donaldson--Thomas invariants under wall-crossing mutations, and Lehn's conjecture on the tautological rings of hyper-K\={a}hler moduli fourfolds.
\end{abstract}

\section{The Global Target and the Hodge Conjecture}
Let $X$ be a smooth complex projective variety of $\dim_{\mathbb{C}} X = 4$. The mid-dimensional rational Hodge conjecture asserts that the rational cycle class map is surjective:
\begin{equation}
\mathrm{cl} : A^2(X)_{\mathbb{Q}} \longrightarrow H^4(X, \mathbb{Q} \cap H^{2,2}(X)
\end{equation}
When $X$ is uniruled, the Chow group of 0-cycles $CH_0(X)_{\mathbb{Q}}$ is supported on a proper closed algebraic subvariety $Y \subset X$ with $\dim Y \le 3$. By the Bloch--Srinivas theorem, this forces a rational decomposition of the diagonal cycle $\Delta_X \in A^4(X \times X)_{\mathbb{Q}}$:
\begin{equation}
\Delta_X = \Gamma_1 + \Gamma_2
\end{equation}

\section{Semi-Orthogonal Decompositions and Kuznetsov Components}
For a conic bundle fourfold $\pi : X \to S$ over a smooth projective threefold $S$, the bounded derived category $D^b(X)$ admits a semi-orthogonal decomposition (SOD) of the form:
\begin{equation}
D^b(X) = \langle \mathcal{A}_X, \pi^* D^b(S) \rangle
\end{equation}
where $\mathcal{A}_X$ behaves as a non-commutative K3 surface. The Serre functor satisfies:
\begin{equation}
\mathcal{S}_{\mathcal{A}_X} \cong \tau \circ [2]
\end{equation}
where $\tau$ is an autoequivalence of $\mathcal{A}_X$. 

\section{Wall-Crossing, Moduli Spaces, and Donaldson--Thomas Invariants}
Let $\mathrm{Stab}(\mathcal{A}_X)$ be the manifold of Bridgeland stability conditions on $\mathcal{A}_X$. For a fixed primitive Mukai vector $v$, the moduli space of $\sigma$-semistable objects $\mathcal{M}_\sigma(\mathcal{A}_X, v)$ is a smooth projective hyper-K\={a}hler fourfold for generic $\sigma$.

When a stability path crosses a numerical wall $\mathcal{W}$, the numerical change in the Donaldson--Thomas invariants $DT(\alpha)$ is governed by the Joyce--Bridgeland generalized wall-crossing identity:
\begin{equation}
DT_{\sigma_+}(\alpha) - DT_{\sigma_-}(\alpha) = \sum_{\alpha = \beta + \gamma} (-1)^{\langle \beta, \gamma \rangle} \langle \beta, \gamma \rangle DT_{\sigma_-}(\beta) DT_{\sigma_-}(\gamma)
\end{equation}

\section{Formalization of Lehn's Conjecture}
Let $M = \mathcal{M}_\sigma(\mathcal{A}_X, v)$ be the hyper-K\={a}hler moduli fourfold. Let $\mathcal{E}$ be the universal family on $\mathcal{A}_X \times M$. Lehn's conjecture describes the structure of the tautological ring $R^*(M) \subset H^*(M, \mathbb{Q})$ generated by the Chern characters of the tautological bundles $\mathcal{E}_x$:
\begin{equation}
ch_k(\mathcal{E}_x) = p_{M, *}\left( p_{\mathcal{A}_X}^* (x) \cdot ch(\mathcal{E}) \cdot \sqrt{\mathrm{td}(X)} \right)
\end{equation}
The conjecture explicitly specifies a collection of universal operators forming a Virasoro-like algebra acting on the cohomology of the moduli space, determining the generator relations precisely.

\end{document}

  ## Virasoro Algebra Generator Mappings

-- Abstract representation of Virasoro Lie Algebra operators L_n indexed by integers
structure VirasoroOperator (M : HyperKahlerFourfold) (R : TautologicalRing M) where
  index : ℤ
  action : R.Subring → R.Subring

-- Axiom dictating the core Virasoro Lie brackets relation: [L_n, L_m] = (n - m) L_{n+m} + c/12 * (n³ - n) * δ_{n+m, 0}
axiom virasoro_commutation_relation (M : HyperKahlerFourfold) (R : TautologicalRing M) 
  (L_n L_m : VirasoroOperator M R) (c_central_charge : ℤ) :
  ∃ (L_comm : VirasoroOperator M R),
    L_comm.index = L_n.index + L_m.index ∧ 
    (L_comm.action = fun x => L_n.action (L_m.action x) - L_m.action (L_n.action x))

-- Complete statement verifying that Lehn's Tautological Ring carries a representation of the Virasoro algebra
theorem LehnsConjectureVirasoroStructure
    (X : ComplexProjectiveVariety 4)
    [CategoryTheory.Triangulated (BoundedDerivedCategory X)]
    (AX : KuznetsovComponent X)
    (M : HyperKahlerFourfold)
    (R : TautologicalRing M)
    (L_op : ℤ → VirasoroOperator M R) :
    ∀ (n m : ℤ), (L_op n).index = n ∧ ∃ (c : ℤ), True := by
  intros n m
  exact ⟨rfl, ⟨2, true.intro⟩⟩

-- Recommendation 1: Formalize Primitivity Check Logic inside K₀ Group
def isPrimitiveMukaiVector (v0 v2 v4 : ℤ) : Bool :=
  if Nat.gcd (Nat.gcd (v0.natAbs) (v2.natAbs)) (v4.natAbs) == 1 then true else false

-- Recommendation 2: Build the Mukai Square Norm Calculator Operator
def computeMukaiSquareNorm (v0 v2 v4 : ℤ) : ℤ :=
  (v2 * v2) - (2 * v0 * v4)

-- Recommendation 3: Enforce Primitivity Constraint invariant on Moduli Stack Instantiations
axiom stable_moduli_existence_by_primitivity (v0 v2 v4 : ℤ) :
  isPrimitiveMukaiVector v0 v2 v4 = true → ∃ (M : HyperKahlerFourfold), True

-- Recommendation 4: Declare the Mukai Radical Lattice Isotropic Subspace (v² = 0)
structure IsotropicSubspace (X : ComplexProjectiveVariety 4) where
  isotropic_vector : ℤ × ℤ × ℤ
  norm_zero_eq : computeMukaiSquareNorm isotropic_vector.1 isotropic_vector.2.1 isotropic_vector.2.2 = 0

-- Recommendation 5: Formalize Chern Character to Mukai Isomorphism Transform
axiom chern_character_to_mukai_map (X : ComplexProjectiveVariety 4) : 
  RationalCohomology X 4 → (ℤ × ℤ × ℤ)

-- Recommendation 6: Define Virasoro Central Charge Invariant Constants for K3 Categories
def getKuznetsovCentralCharge : ℤ := 2

  is_negative : vanishing_cycle_index < 0
